- **关联关键词**: Agent, Robotics, ComputerVision

#### 研究背景与动机

《Beyond Prediction Gain: LASR as a Metric for Action-Grounded Latents in World Models》归入 世界模型、具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：GenEval, UniVLA, VBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

World models that support planning, control, and interpretable reasoning require latent representations that genuinely encode action. Yet the large-scale video data from which such models are learned overwhelmingly lack action annotations. Latent-action models infer action-like variables from observation sequences, enabling action-aware learning from unlabeled videos or trajectories. A fundamental concern, however, remains: a latent that improves future prediction may simply encode the current state, episode progress, task identity, or other predictive shortcuts. Existing evaluation practices---reconstruction loss, rollout fidelity, action recovery, or downstream performance---cannot isolate this distinction. We introduce the Latent Action Support Ratio (LASR), a dimensionless metric that measures, under offline evaluation on held-out data, whether a learned latent provides predictive support for action-related targets beyond the current state and non-action alternatives. LASR provides two complementary summaries: GeoLASR captures typical support, while WorstLASR identifies the weakest case. We instantiate the LASR benchmark with recorded actions and corresponding physical state changes as targets, episode-held-out splits, one predictor type for all comparisons, and positive and negative controls. Experiments on recorded robot manipulation data and a matched multi-task panel demonstrate that LASR detects known action information, rejects representative non-action shortcuts, and enables graded comparisons among imperfect latent representations. We further use LASR to train and audit a lightweight residual adapter, obtaining descriptively better point estimates whose uncertainty is assessed with paired comparisons. LASR thus provides a principled offline protocol for measuring action-related predictive support in world-model latents.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Joint World Action Models through Differentiable Trajectory Optimization》归入 世界模型、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：GCRL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Joint world action models (WAMs) generate future actions and states together, modeling world-action trajectories rather than actions alone. However, trajectory imitation does not ensure that these trajectories are task-effective or consistent with dynamics. We present \textbf{world action trajectory optimization (WATO)}, which optimizes generated actions and future states jointly for control. WATO uses both as references in a dynamics-constrained trajectory optimization problem and differentiates the task loss through the optimized trajectory to train the generator. This makes generated trajectories task-aware and dynamics-consistent while using trajectory optimization only during training. At deployment, WATO directly generates candidate trajectories and ranks them with a world model, without iterative trajectory optimization. Across $7$ visual control environments, WATO improves average success from $79.9\%$ for the imitation joint WAM to $91.7\%$, outperforming strong model-based, goal-conditioned RL, and generative control baselines. Compared with cross-entropy method (CEM), WATO is on average $11.5\times$ faster and uses $600.5\times$ fewer world model evaluations at deployment.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Learning Latent Goal Costs for Visual World-Model Planning with an Expanding Curriculum》归入 世界模型、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Image-goal planning with a frozen visual world model depends on how predicted observations are scored against the goal. Mean-squared error (MSE) between embeddings from a pretrained visual encoder can provide a useful signal near a goal, yet favor states across an obstacle that are visually similar to the goal and difficult to reach. We fit a small residual adapter on top of frozen I-JEPA embeddings to improve this latent goal cost. Starting near each goal, a planner collects trajectories from progressively wider regions of initial states. The adapter learns from the order of observations along successful trajectories, with additional comparisons inferred from failed rollouts. No task-specific progress labels are used for fitting, so the procedure can be applied to other goal-reaching tasks. DotWall and PointMaze are used in this work. In paired 50-case evaluations with identical starts, goals, world models, and planner settings, success increases from 54% to 98% in DotWall and from 82% to 96% in PointMaze. On 16 unseen DotWall layout-goal cases, the adapter fitted on one fixed layout improves agreement between latent-cost ordering and shortest legal-path ordering from 71.9% to 85.8%. The results indicate that planning experience can improve a frozen visual representation's goal cost without retraining the encoder or the world model.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Training Reasoning Models in Simulation to Natively Use Real Robots》归入 机器人、具身智能、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AgenticRoboRL, DigiRL, ERQA, GPQA, IFBench, LiveCodeBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robot-use agents (RUAs) have emerged as a promising approach to general-purpose robot control. However, strong performance has so far been achieved only by frontier proprietary models such as GPT-6 Astra and Claude Fable 5.2, whereas open-weight models remain substantially less capable. We show that an open-weight 27B reasoning model can be trained to use real robots natively through reinforcement learning, entirely in simulation and without demonstrations. Like a computer-use agent (CUA), the model observes the environment, reasons in a chain of thought, and generates the next end-effector pose, without any harness. We build a Post-training Engine for Agentic Robotics (PEAR) and use it to train the model on simulated robot tasks at scale with robot-use reinforcement learning (RURL), mirroring how frontier models acquire computer use. We find that the skills acquired in simulation generalize well beyond the training tasks: they transfer to an unseen robot, to the real world, and to novel instructions. After training on LIBERO and RoboDojo in simulation, the model succeeds in 60% of our trials on a real dual-arm robot, whereas the base model succeeds in none. On LIBERO, training raises the success rate from 1.6% to 74.4%, approaching GPT-6 Astra and GPT-6.1 Sol at maximum reasoning effort. Compared with $π_{0.5}$, a vision-language-action model fine-tuned on demonstrations, our model is substantially more robust to changes in the task itself. On LIBERO-Pro, $π_{0.5}$ succeeds in only 0.75% of episodes when assigned a new task in a familiar scene whereas our model succeeds in 57.4%. Finally, the model retains its general capabilities, with no measurable degradation on nine reasoning, coding and vision benchmarks. These results highlight robot-use reinforcement learning as a promising component of foundation-model post-training.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Modeling the Frictional Contact of Continuum Robots with Environments through Linear Complementarity Problem》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Accurate friction modeling in continuum robots is essential for precise motion control and path planning. However, current approaches make predictions only for the mechanism's internal friction that is limited by the boundary forces of the Coulomb friction cone, which fail to characterize the static friction and motion hysteresis. In this paper, we propose a novel friction model based on complementarity constraints under the Coulomb friction assumption. This model predicts various friction effects caused by environmental interactions, including both static and sliding frictions and hysteresis behavior, as well as the corresponding robot deformation induced by these interactions, which are crucial for the practical motion planning and control of continuum robots. Additionally, real-time implementation of this model can be achieved, meeting the demand for computational efficiency in robotic applications. Experimental validations demonstrated that this model is able to reduce the maximum tip position error from 45.4 mm in the frictionless model to 5.85 mm in our friction-included model with a 287.9 mm-long tendon-driven continuum robot.

</details>

《Disturbance-Aware Informative Planning in Spatiotemporal Environments》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Autonomous robots are widely used for persistent monitoring of spatiotemporal environments, where quantities of interest (QoI) change across space and time and require repeated measurements. In practice, robot motion can be affected by external disturbances that vary across the environment. When such external disturbances are not considered in trajectory optimization, it can lead to inaccurate predictions of robot motion and, consequently, the information acquired about the QoI during execution. However, estimating disturbances throughout the environment can be inefficient, as disturbances in regions with little overlap with monitoring trajectories have limited impact on monitoring performance. We therefore propose a two-stage informative planning framework for unknown, spatially varying but time-invariant disturbances, combining informed disturbance sampling with disturbance-aware trajectory optimization. First, we identify where QoI measurements are needed and generate alternative paths to these locations using the environment geometry. We then prioritize disturbance measurements based on their expected reduction of disturbance uncertainty along these paths. Second, rather than optimizing information acquisition based on nominal robot motion, we incorporate the learned disturbance belief into trajectory optimization to account for its effects on robot motion and information acquisition. Across four simulation scenarios, the proposed method reduces disturbance-learning time by 25.0--52.3 percent compared with environment-wide estimation and identifies paths that appear costly under nominal dynamics but become preferable when disturbances are considered.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Toward Resilient Self-Driving Laboratories through Telerobotic Interventions》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Self-driving laboratories (SDLs) accelerate scientific research by integrating robotics, automated experimentation, and machine learning to execute experiments autonomously. Biology and chemistry have adopted these systems. However, increasing SDL complexity heightens susceptibility to unexpected faults in automated workflows, highlighting the need for efficient recovery mechanisms. In this study, we investigate telerobotic interventions to enhance SDL resilience against unexpected failures. We developed a teleoperation interface enabling human operators to resolve disruptions during automated tasks. The interface enables precise control of a six-degree-of-freedom (DOF) robotic arm (UR3e) using a 3D mouse (3Dconnexion SpaceMouse Pro). Two velocity-based controller strategies were evaluated: (i) a coupled mode mapping all six DOFs to the 3D mouse's central knob and (ii) a decoupled mode separating translational and rotational motions. To evaluate the framework, teleoperators intervened across three SDL fault scenarios. In the first study, comparative trials showed that the decoupled controller was associated with shorter completion times and reduced rotational motion. In a second study, teleoperators performed an aiming task with and without reference-path guidance. Users were more target-driven and risk-tolerant without guidance but showed behavioral differences under different guidance strategies. These findings suggest that controller configuration and reference-path design influence operator performance during SDL fault recovery, informing teleoperation interface optimization. This work demonstrates the potential feasibility of teleoperation as a human-in-the-loop recovery mechanism for SDL workflows, combining robotic precision with human adaptability and problem-solving. These findings also provide a foundation for future studies in shared autonomy and semi-autonomous intervention.

</details>

《From AI-Enabled Remote Patient Monitoring Toward Agentic Robotics: Embodiment, Governance, and Care Integration》归入 机器人、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：该工作聚焦 Artificial Intelligence 方向中的具体问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：可见文本中未给出明确实验数字或完整对比表。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

AI-enabled remote patient monitoring is evolving beyond passive data collection toward agentic AI systems that interpret patient states, coordinate care, and initiate action across domestic and clinical settings. This structured scoping review finds that mature agentic care has not yet emerged: Most systems remain human led, while stronger decision-action coupling and embodied AI remain largely confined to prototypes and pilots. Healthcare robotics makes the transition from monitoring to action especially visible by translating AI-generated recommendations and decisions into speech, movement, prompting, physical assistance, escalation, and care coordination, making embodiment, clinical integration, human oversight, and governance fundamental design requirements. Further progress requires safe and interoperable care architectures in which intelligent components operate through clinically validated and accountable workflows. We propose agentic robotics as a future direction for supervised systems embedded in the care ecosystem, connecting AI agents and robots with clinicians, caregivers, and patients through auditable workflows that can be interrupted and remain subject to human oversight and explicit AI governance.

</details>

《CS-MPC: Contraction-Certified Sampling-Based MPC for Nonlinear Systems Under Uncertainty》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：给出系统化方法或工具；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

We present contraction-certified sampling-based model predictive control (CS-MPC) for tracking and safety of nonlinear robotic systems under parametric uncertainty, disturbances, and safety constraints that are revealed online. CS-MPC uses a contraction-certified tracking feedback inside every sampled rollout, which concentrates the rollouts around the reference, and an execution layer that verifies the applied input using a quantile-tightened discrete-time control barrier function condition. We derive a verification procedure for nonlinear contraction of the feedback-closed dynamics, and we prove a finite-sample stochastic stability bound and a mission-level probabilistic safety guarantee. We validate CS-MPC in simulation on a rover, a quadrotor, and an asteroid landing. When compared with standard model predictive path integral (MPPI) control, CS-MPC attains the same tracking error with 7 to 20 times fewer rollouts for the rover, and a 2 to 4 times lower tracking error for the quadrotor, with no safety violations in 200 Monte Carlo missions.

</details>

《Envisioning Agentic Robotics: A New Era of Robotic Systems》归入 机器人、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

The release of GPT-6 Astra marks the emergence of a new paradigm in robotics: agentic robotics. Within weeks, frontier models with agentic harnesses matched or exceeded task-specific policies on many embodied benchmarks. Instead of training a dedicated policy for each family of tasks, an agentic robotic system applies a foundation model to open-world embodied tasks. It operates in a closed loop: observing the environment, interpreting user intent, reasoning about the task, invoking skills and tools, verifying outcomes, and adapting its strategy. Despite these advances, the community has yet to converge on a shared definition, a reliable evaluation framework, or a research roadmap. We argue that Embodied System 2, realized by a foundation model together with an embodied harness, forms the decision-making core of agentic robotics. Its central challenge is to translate general-purpose reasoning into effective and verifiable actions under physical constraints such as limited reaction time, mechanical limits, unpredictable environment changes, and irreversible consequences. Motivated by this perspective, we propose an evaluation framework that measures both model capabilities and closed-loop system performance. Furthermore, we identify the principles that carry over from digital to physical agents and the challenges that must be addressed to achieve reliable physical autonomy. On top of the Embodied System 2 and the evaluation framework, we envision a research roadmap toward sustained autonomy: the ability to perform work safely and reliably with progressively broader responsibility, from executing individual primitives to fulfilling standing roles.

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Reaching a goal state in a complex environment often requires a robot to overcome challenging terrain and obstacles. Which routes are accessible depends on the robot's physical design, the control policy it uses, and how it modifies its surroundings. We present a kinodynamic rapidly-exploring random tree (RRT) planner that uses physics simulation to exploit skills encoded in the control policy, which allows it to find a path in the scene with respect to the robot's capabilities. The planner samples states from a reduced state space in 3D around the scene and expands the RRT toward each sample using policy rollouts. We guide sampling with a preplanned path for a simple mass-point model to focus exploration on promising regions leading toward the goal state, reducing planning time by up to 63%. Simulation experiments with quadrupeds, humanoids, and wheeled robots demonstrate that our approach supports drop-in integration of various robots and control policies, exploiting their physical and policy capabilities without modifying the underlying planning algorithm. Predefined environmental modifications, such as placing a plank across a gap to build a bridge, will automatically be picked up by the planner as the robot's reachable space expands.

</details>

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Large language and vision-language models have substantially advanced robotic behavior planning from natural-language instructions and visual observations. However, safe manipulation in tabletop environments remains challenging because the safety constraints required for a task depend on the surrounding scene and may not be explicitly specified in the instruction. We propose a safety-aware behavior planning framework based on an explicit Constraint Space that stores reusable textual safety rules. Rather than retrieving rules solely by semantic similarity, we formulate constraint selection as task- and environment-conditioned applicability learning. A lightweight query-conditioned dual-tower model identifies the constraints applicable to the current context, which are then provided together with the original task and image to a multimodal behavior planner. To handle incorrect constraint selections and previously unseen hazards, we introduce a multimodal Safety Inspector that evaluates generated primitive plans, reconstructs the complete applicable constraint set, and provides corrective planning feedback. Validated inspection feedback is further used for test-time adaptation of constraint applicability and parameter-efficient adaptation of the Safety Inspector through LoRA, while newly identified constraints are incorporated into the Constraint Space. The resulting framework integrates explicit safety knowledge representation,context-aware constraint selection, corrective safety inspection, and test-time knowledge adaptation for safety-aware robotic behavior planning.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Per-Agent-Safe Navigation of Globally Driven Robot Collectives via Affine Field Design》归入 机器人、大模型、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、大模型、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Safe navigation of robot collectives requires coordinating motion and deformation so that the collective can traverse constrained environments while ensuring each robot's safety. Existing controllers for swarmalator collectives largely rely on local control, where each robot receives an individual command, which causes the control and communication requirements to grow with the number of agents. We introduce a broadcast control formulation in which a single spatially affine field governs both the motion and deformation of the collective. The scalar component of the field regulates collective size, and its spatial gradient generates collective translation through non-reciprocal interactions. Both components enter the collective dynamics directly, which allows per-robot wall, actuation, and cohesion constraints to be enforced using control barrier functions within a single quadratic program for the whole collective. The resulting controller continuously adapts the collective size to the local environment, and enables safe passage through corridors narrower than the unconstrained collective without pre-planned resizing. We further derive how the available actuation budget determines the reachable speed and minimum passable corridor width, and we demonstrate that two independently driven collectives can simultaneously traverse a shared junction using distinct fixed phase labels without inter-collective coordination.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/机器人/Alleviating_Interview_Stress_Effects_of_Robot_Persona_and_Reactive_Backchanneling_on_High-Pressure_Mock_Interviews|Alleviating Interview Stress: Effects of Robot Persona and Reactive Backchanneling on High-Pressure Mock Interviews]]

![[assets/2611.17830_first_page.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.17830](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17830)

- **作者**: Haochen Yang, Zhuoran Gao, Geqing Tao, Yusi Dong, Pei-Luen Patrick Rau
- **cs 子类**: cs.HC
- **归属领域**: 机器人

#### 研究背景与动机

《Alleviating Interview Stress: Effects of Robot Persona and Reactive Backchanneling on High-Pressure Mock Interviews》归入 机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Human-Computer Interaction 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：围绕论文提出的建模、算法或系统设计进行实验验证。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

High-pressure job interviews induce anxiety and impair performance. Existing mock training suffers from a trade-off among cost, standardization, and physical presence. This study investigated how a robot interviewer's verbal persona (supportive vs. challenging) and nonverbal backchanneling (active vs. negative) affect users' psychological states and facial expressions in a simulated interview. In a 2x2 between-subjects experiment (N=28), participants interacted with a NAO robot. Subjective measures (state anxiety, self-efficacy, social presence) and objective facial emotion recognition were collected. Results showed that a supportive persona reduced task load, increased perceived robot warmth and mind perception, and decreased perceived surveillance and robot discomfort. Active backchanneling increased non-neutral (p = .021) and anxiety-tension facial affect (p = .048). A significant interaction effect between persona and backchanneling was found on social presence (p = .045). The subjective-objective consistency was 67.9%, indicating complementary measurement modalities. The findings provide preliminary support for the social buffering hypothesis in human-robot interaction.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Resource Management and Trajectory Optimization for UAV-IRS Assisted Maritime Edge Computing Networks》归入 机器人、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Networking and Internet Architecture 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

With the exponential growth of maritime wireless devices and the rapid development of maritime applications, traditional maritime communication networks encounter communication and computation limitations in supporting computation-intensive and latency-critical tasks. Edge computing and Intelligent Reflecting Surface (IRS) have emerged as promising techniques to improve communication and computation services for maritime devices with limited computation capabilities and battery capacity. This paper studies an IRS Mounted on Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UIRS) assisted maritime edge computing network, in which the UIRS is deployed to assist the transmission from Unmanned Surface Vehicles (USVs) to the edge server via Non-Orthogonal Multiple Access (NOMA) protocol. We propose a resource management and trajectory optimization scheme by jointly optimizing subslot duration, offloading ratios, transmit power, edge computation capability allocation, UIRS phase shifts and UIRS trajectory, aiming at minimizing the overall energy consumption. Since the non-convex nature of the optimization problem, we propose a two-layered method by decomposing the original problem into two subproblems. The top-layered subproblem is solved by the Semi-Definite Relaxation (SDR) method and the underlying-layered subproblem is solved by the Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG) algorithm. Numerical results demonstrate that our proposed scheme can effectively and efficiently reduce overall energy consumption.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Multimodal Person Concept Model for Person Identification under Varying Observation Conditions》归入 机器人、大模型、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、大模型、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Service robots in domestic and office environments must identify users to provide personalized services based on their preferences and previous interactions. This is challenging because only a few observations may be available for learning, while observation conditions change owing to appearance, environmental noise, and utterance content. To address this challenge, this study proposes a multimodal person concept model that learns person concepts from a small number of observations, without person labels, and enables robust identification under changing conditions. The model employs a nonparametric Bayesian generative model based on the Chinese restaurant process and integrates visual, acoustic, and linguistic features extracted using Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP) and Contrastive Language-Audio Pretraining (CLAP), followed by principal component analysis (PCA). We evaluated six participants under eight conditions in which appearance changes, additive noise, and transcription errors were introduced individually and in combination. Averaged over the eight conditions, the proposed model achieved an identification accuracy of 0.89 while inferring the number of person concepts from the observations, which is comparable to k-means clustering that was supplied with the true number of participants (0.88). We also examined PCA dimensionality, modality availability, and variation strength, and demonstrated the model on a Toyota Human Support Robot. These results show that multimodal person concept learning supports robust person identification under changing conditions, and that identification can succeed even when the number of concepts is not correctly recovered. The project page and demonstration video are available at https://okitamisan01.github.io/person_concept/.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《INC-SRv6: In-Network Route Exploration and Fast Failover Using Segment Routing Headers in Dynamic UAV Networks》归入 机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Distributed, Parallel, and Cluster Computing 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：该工作聚焦 Distributed, Parallel, and Cluster Computing 方向中的具体问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：围绕论文提出的建模、算法或系统设计进行实验验证。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) networks require a network provisioning mechanism that guarantees delivery and latency quality of service (QoS) under dynamic conditions in next-generation airborne mesh networks by continuously learning and repairing multi-hop paths under rapid link turnover without flooding the control plane. This paper presents INC-SRv6, an in-network computing routing mechanism that repurposes the IPv6 Segment Routing Header (SRH) and its Segments Left (SL) pointer into a self-learning, fast-failover distributed control plane. Rather than treating the SRH as a static source-committed vector, INC-SRv6 uses the active SL pointer to partition the segment list in flight: slots ahead of SL provide capacity for hop-by-hop next-hop recording, while the suffix behind SL serves as an in-band multi-destination reachability vector. Through this dual-zone header discipline, transit routers extract distance-weighted reachability updates from arriving suffixes, gate admission by single-pass confidence with bounded binary probing, advance or reverse SL deterministically, and restore pre-transmission SRH state for local failover upon link loss. Simulations across 200-node 3D UAV networks show that under high-speed flight and repeated 20% relay outages, INC-SRv6 improves packet delivery over reactive routing (92.12% versus 89.12%) while cutting 95th-percentile tail delay from 121.26 to 8.84 ms and control packets by 35.8%, with a 4-byte compact uSID stride model reducing SRv6 control bytes by over 52%.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《AI-guided robotic in situ fabrication of curvature- and tissue-adaptive wireless bioelectronics》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：该工作聚焦 Robotics 方向中的具体问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Personalized bioelectronic devices for wound therapy require not only conformal fabrication on complex body surfaces but also functional adaptation of their electrical characteristics to individual wound geometries. Here, we present an AI-guided robotic in situ printing platform for fabricating wireless wound electrotherapy devices directly on transparent wound dressings. The system integrates a 3D vision camera, a six-degree-of-freedom robotic arm, direct-ink-writing, and geometry-aware RF design optimization. AI-based wound segmentation is used to determine wound dimensions and local surface curvature, while the reconstructed three-dimensional geometry is incorporated into the electromagnetic design of the receiving coil and interdigitated electrodes. In contrast to conventional approaches that use surface geometry only for trajectory generation, the proposed method accounts for both anatomical curvature and tissue dielectric loading to compensate for geometry-induced shifts in resonant behavior. A printable silver ink was characterized in terms of rheology, dimensional reproducibility, electrical resistance, and electromechanical stability, and was used to fabricate multilayer wireless devices directly on curved polyurethane dressings. Wireless power transfer, impedance characteristics, and electric-field delivery were evaluated experimentally and numerically. Finally, robotic in situ fabrication was demonstrated on an anthropomorphic tissue-equivalent forearm phantom, where the measured dielectric properties of the phantom were incorporated into the RF optimization, enabling resonant operation near the target frequency of 25 MHz. This work establishes a perception-to-design-to-fabrication framework for geometry-adaptive wireless bioelectronics and provides a technological foundation for future physical-AI-enabled manufacturing of personalized on-body electrotherapy devices.

</details>

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：MetaWorld, Real-World, ResNet, U-Net。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Visuomotor imitation learning has become a predominant paradigm for real-world robotic manipulation, yet it remains data-inefficient and brittle to task-irrelevant visual shifts. Pre-trained visual representations promise to ease this burden by distilling priors from internet-scale data, but the ``right'' representation for robotics remains unresolved: existing studies span broad but sparse task distributions and reach conclusions that vary and even conflict, pointing to strong task and setting dependence. We examine this question by bringing a magnifying glass to a common contact-rich manipulation task: insertion. We compare representations from widely used vision foundation models against an explicit contact-centric representation on two closely related insertion tasks, perturb each along controlled axes of occlusion and physical tolerance, and test whether conclusions transfer to the real world. Task dependence proves to be more subtle than previous, broadly scoped evaluations revealed: the best representation differs even between our near-identical tasks, and small changes shift trends in unpredictable ways. Despite this variability, results across simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate the distinct value of spatially-grounded, and in particular, contact-centric information for contact-rich policy learning, while underscoring how difficult it is to estimate such cues reliably under realistic visual complexity and sensor noise. We hope these findings motivate further study of representations in contact-rich manipulation, along with advances in contact perception and its integration into visuomotor policy learning.

</details>

- **作者**: Jiaxin Huang, Chengxing Lin, Xiaoyu Luo, Sirui Chen, Jingyu Guo, Aolin Li, Kairui Chen, Rui Zhen, Cheng Wen, Kerui Gu, Shunyao Wang, Zhihui Peng...

#### 研究背景与动机

《X-Nav Technical Report: Real-World Long-Horizon Indoor-to-Outdoor Navigation on a Humanoid Robot》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World, X-Nav-Sim。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Long-horizon navigation from indoors to outdoors remains an open problem for humanoid robots: one instruction covers rooms, doors and roads, and the camera moves with the gait. Worken, AGIBOT's measure of productive robot work, depends on robots that reach their workplaces autonomously. We present X-Nav, to our knowledge the first system to take a bipedal humanoid through a language-instructed long-horizon mission from indoors to outdoors. X-Nav covers a pipeline from data to deployment. Its data engine collects navigation data in open-source scenes, in digital twins of deployment sites and on real robots. Its navigation model, MELIOR, takes only RGB images from the robot's built-in camera and comes in two versions: MELIOR-A predicts actions, and MELIOR-N predicts a navigation goal. Its agentic workflow, X-Navarch, turns a brief human instruction into sub-tasks and deploys MELIOR on the robot with explicit target navigation and obstacle avoidance. On the Lingxi X2, X-Nav completes a mission of about a hundred metres from an office lobby to a store in a neighbouring building; LiDAR and odometry are used for target approach and low-level control. The mission is completed with both versions of MELIOR: the robot follows a change of destination and walks through a door from the lobby to the road, and with X-Navarch it also passes static and dynamic obstacles on the route. During deployment, MELIOR is adapted with data collected in a digital twin of the site; the adaptation raises its closed-loop success rate in the twin, and the adapted model is then deployed on the robot at the real site. In addition, MELIOR achieves competitive results on the R2R-CE and RxR-CE benchmarks.

</details>

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：围绕论文提出的建模、算法或系统设计进行实验验证。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robots with weak motors must ride their natural dynamics rather than fight them. We swing up a direct-drive planar two-link robot, whose motors deliver only about 6\,\% of its shoulder gravity torque, along its nonlinear normal modes, two-dimensional invariant manifolds consisting of periodic orbits. On a non-strict mode, injecting energy necessarily moves the state off the manifold, so pumping and stabilization compete for the same bounded torque. We split the input torque into a power-neutral steering component and a scalar pumping input, the only component that changes the energy. The allocation then becomes a linear program in two variables: maximize pumping subject to the torque limits and one control-Lyapunov constraint. The constraint uses the model-based rate of the error, so no stabilizing feedback gain is needed. Experiments in hardware show swing-ups along both modes and compare the allocation with summing-and-clipping and with prioritized stabilization.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Grounding Object-to-Object Hazard Reasoning into 3D Risk Fields for Task-Conditioned Safety》归入 机器人、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Safety in robotics does not solely depend on geometric constraints, but also on semantics: the given task, environment properties, and interactions between objects in the scene. We present a framework based on vision-language models (VLMs) to infer task- and scene-dependent safety constraints for robot manipulation. We provide the VLM a structured safety tool based on an RGB-D observation and a natural-language task instruction that first decides whether the task should be rejected since its execution may result in a non-recoverable hazardous outcome. Otherwise, it identifies preventable object-pair hazards and specifies the spatial relations that must be avoided. Open-vocabulary segmentation and depth information are then used to ground the detected objects in 3D and construct continuous risk fields that represent the corresponding hazardous regions. These fields are integrated with an obstacle avoidance module that corrects nominal robot motions during execution while accounting for the risk field. We evaluate the framework on preventable hazards, tasks requiring rejection, and safe variants in simulation and the real-world. Project website: https://vlm-hazard-riskfield.github.io

</details>

![[assets/2611.17549_figure.png|800]]

- **作者**: Deepu Krishnareddy, Mohammad Haziq Khan, Michael Danner, Matthias Rätsch
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《Talk to Act: A Synthetic Multi-Turn Dialogue Benchmark for Human Intent Extraction and Action Planning in Robotics》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Multi-turn conversational human-robot interaction (HRI) continues to be a challenging problem in robotics, as many existing systems rely on rigid, command-driven interfaces that can be unintuitive for users. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) provide a promising direction, given their ability to process natural language, generate structured outputs, and support tool-based reasoning, all of which are relevant for interpreting user intent in robotic settings. However, progress in this area is constrained by the limited availability of datasets and benchmarks that capture the nuances of real-world human communication, such as multi-turn dialogue, ambiguity, and context-dependent requests. In this work, we present a synthetically generated multi-turn dialogue dataset for HRI, annotated with action intents, object references, and task plans across a range of scenarios and user personas. Our primary focus is on evaluating the capability of small, open-weight language models for robotic tasks, particularly in settings where local deployment and efficiency are important. To this end, we frame intent extraction as a tool-calling problem where each predicted action corresponds to a structured function call with arguments grounded in the dialogue and benchmark models ranging from 270M to 8B parameters on their ability to perform intent extraction and action planning without task-specific fine-tuning. Our results show that these models can achieve reasonable performance on the task even at relatively small scales and without any task-specific fine-tuning. This suggests that compact, locally deployable models already provide a useful baseline for conversational robotic systems. We expect that task-specific fine-tuning and more realistic training data will enhance performance further and make such models increasingly suitable for real-world robotic applications.

</details>

- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.17492

- **作者**: Liheng Zeng, Hanzhang Jia, Yiran Li, Yi Gao, Bo Ma
- **cs 子类**: cs.IT, cs.LG
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人

#### 研究背景与动机

《PF-Exec: Large-Scale UAV Relay Load Balancing with an Anchored Potential Direction Decider and a Per-Dimension Gain-Learning Executor》归入 机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Information Theory 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Relay load balancing in large-scale UAV networks requires joint decisions on association, bandwidth, power and hovering position, while the dimension grows with the number of UAVs and each UAV sees only its local state. This paper splits the decision into two layers. An anchored potential direction decider emits a closed-form target at every instant and returns its direction only. A gain-learning executor maps a local observation into a positive gain per dimension, composes gain and target, and clips the action against the physical single-step bound; the two layers form PF-Exec. The unit gain is the default start, so the executor reduces to the pure potential policy before training. The load is first-order homogeneous in bandwidth, making the bandwidth target closed form, and the position target is the demand-weighted centroid of the served users. The system uses a two-hop access and millimetre-wave backhaul link with soft association, a 3GPP UMa-AV channel with two shadowing layers, an OU-type social-force crowd and a quadrotor physical layer. Evaluation spans all fleet sizes, two crowd scenarios, independent seeds, the published baselines in their own settings and a degraded-information line. In the highest-pressure burst setting the fixed-step comparison breaks down on part of the seeds, losing fairness and mean load at once, while PF-Exec holds fairness near one. Among the ten comparison methods only the greedy heuristic survives that setting; its load variance stays far higher. Under stale global state and position noise the fairness index stays high at both largest-scale scenarios. Flight energy is nearly identical between the learned and the fixed-step executor, and three constants are fixed once across all settings. The convergence properties of both layers, from the truncated gain iteration to the perturbed tracking bound, are proved in the companion analysis.

</details>

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：GOAT-Bench, Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

A rescue robot entering a building with thirty seconds on the clock should sprint toward the single most promising room; given ten minutes, it should instead explore a broader tour. Yet vision-language-navigation (VLN) models are typically evaluated under a fixed episode limit that the policy never observes, even though time-critical missions require behavior to adapt to the available budget. We study budget-conditioned object search: given a target category and an explicit action budget, an agent must find as many distinct target instances as possible before the budget expires. We investigate how to supervise VLNs for this task by comparing a privileged TSP expert, which plans near-optimal routes using the full map and true target locations, with a room-level Bellman expert that maximizes expected return under partial observability using high-level cross-scene semantic priors. Despite the TSP expert achieving substantially higher expert performance, its demonstrations are less effective for learning exploration: on held-out HM3D scenes, the Bellman-supervised VLN consistently outperforms the TSP-supervised VLN, with the advantage becoming more pronounced at longer budgets. At 2000 steps (500m), the Bellman-supervised VLN finds 71.4% of reachable target instances, compared with 65.5% for the TSP-supervised VLN and 44.8% for the pretrained VLN. These results highlight a distinction between optimal routing and optimal exploration: effective exploration supervision should optimize decisions under partial observability rather than imitate routes that are optimal only once the environment is fully known.

</details>

![[assets/2611.17438_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.17438](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17438)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.17438
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Real-Time_Diffusion_Relighting_as_a_Neural_Sensor_Closing_the_Low-Light_Domain_Gap_for_Robotic_Perception|Real-Time Diffusion Relighting as a Neural Sensor: Closing the Low-Light Domain Gap for Robotic Perception]]
- **作者**: Shengjie Xu, Alexander Gao, Anshul Rai, Dae Yeol Lee, Guan-Ming Su, Ming C. Lin
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV
- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人
- **相关性评分**: 0.8（加权：机器人 0.8）
- **关联关键词**: Robotics, EmbodiedAI, ComputerVision

#### 研究背景与动机

《Real-Time Diffusion Relighting as a Neural Sensor: Closing the Low-Light Domain Gap for Robotic Perception》归入 机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：DiLightNet, MambaSFLNet, Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Low-light conditions severely degrade robotic perception: object detectors trained on daylight imagery are prone to failure under dark and adverse weather conditions that fall outside of their training distribution. Furthermore, from an efficiency standpoint, existing Low-Light Image Enhancement (LLIE) methods are designed for offline processing and are therefore impractical to integrate into live control loops. To address these issues, we propose to embed a Generative Neural Sensor within the control loop that treats illumination as a generative prior rather than a signal processing problem, enabling task-relevant semantic recovery. We further propose an Real-Time Perceptual Architecture that decouples high-latency semantic recovery from low-latency robotic control. Rather than blocking the control loop, our system runs a large-scale image-to-image diffusion model in a background thread, and is activated only when a drop in detection confidence signals a perceptual uncertainty event, significantly reducing computational overhead. Our method sets a new state-of-the-art performance on the ExDark low-light benchmark with 87.1% mAP, and under adverse weather conditions, significantly boosts detection rates over competing methods. On a navigation task in a closed-loop autonomous driving simulator (CARLA), we achieve a 100% lap completion rate, establishing a simulation-based proof-of-concept for integrating high-latency generative models with standard robotic control autonomy stacks.

</details>

- **arXiv**: [2611.15781](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15781)

#### 研究背景与动机

《Prediction-Error-Tightened MPC for Socially Compliant Crowd Navigation》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；设计端到端框架；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Autonomous navigation of mobile robots in crowded environments, while preserving pedestrians' psychological comfort, requires distinguishing pedestrians from other obstacles, predicting their future motion, and accounting for uncertainty in the predicted trajectories of both pedestrians and the robot. This work extends a previous MPC framework by integrating obstacle and pedestrian detection, Koopman-based motion prediction, and online prediction-error estimation directly within the collision-avoidance mechanism. To reduce collision-avoidance complexity in crowded scenarios, nearby obstacles are aggregated, thereby reducing the number of collision-avoidance constraints. Simulation studies evaluate the prediction-error margins and the computational benefits of semantics-aware aggregation. In a paired 300-seed closed-loop comparison with a moving generic obstacle, Markov residual tightening reduces the fraction of runs with physical contact from $10.0\%$ to $1.33\%$, at a mean travel-time cost of $0.68$ s. In a separate crowd-navigation semantic ablation, the offline robot-footprint/personal-space overlap occupies $2.11\%$ of observed time with pedestrian-aware handling versus $22.84\%$ with generic-obstacle handling.

</details>
